(function () {
  try {
    document.documentElement.classList.add('csm-cookie-embed');
  } catch (e) {}
})();
</script><script data-consentmo-data-src-bootstrap>
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__consentmoDataSrcBootstrapInit) return;
  window.__consentmoDataSrcBootstrapInit = true;

  var DATA_SRC_SELECTOR = [
    'script[data-src*="consentmo_cookie_consent"]',
    'script[data-src*="gdpr_cookie_consent.min"]',
    'script[data-src*="gdrp_cookie_consent_unlimited"]'
  ].join(',');

  function activateConsentmoDataSrcScripts() {
    if (!window.Shopify || !window.Shopify.shop) return;
    document.querySelectorAll(DATA_SRC_SELECTOR).forEach(function (scriptEl) {
      // getAttribute: src="" must not count as "already has src" (.src === page URL).
      if (scriptEl.getAttribute('src')) return;
      var url = scriptEl.getAttribute('data-src');
      if (!url) return;
      if (scriptEl.getAttribute('type') === 'text/plain') {
        scriptEl.removeAttribute('type');
      }
      scriptEl.src = url;
    });
  }
  function scheduleActivation() {
    activateConsentmoDataSrcScripts();
    // Retry on next task so other DCL handlers (lazy-loaders) can finish setting Shopify.shop / data-src first.
    setTimeout(activateConsentmoDataSrcScripts, 0);
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', scheduleActivation, { once: true });
  } else {
    scheduleActivation();
  }
})();
</script>
